"        // Diagnostic bone logging\n        try {\n          const testBones = [\"leftUpperArm\", \"leftLowerArm\", \"leftHand\", \"rightUpperArm\", \"rightLowerArm\", \"rightHand\", \"head\", \"neck\", \"spine\", \"chest\"];\n          const normalizedNodes: any = {};\n          const rawNodes: any = {};\n          testBones.forEach(b => {\n            normalizedNodes[b] = vrm.humanoid?.getNormalizedBoneNode(b as any) ? \"FOUND\" : \"NULL\";\n            rawNodes[b] = vrm.humanoid?.getRawBoneNode(b as any) ? \"FOUND\" : \"NULL\";\n          });\n          \n          const logPayload = {\n            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n            vrmVersion: vrm.meta?.version || \"unknown\",\n            vrmMetaName: vrm.meta?.name || \"unknown\",\n            normalizedBones: normalizedNodes,\n            rawBones: rawNodes,\n            humanoidExists: !!vrm.humanoid,\n            autoUpdateBonesValue: vrm.humanoid?.autoUpdateHumanBones,\n          };\n          \n          const logStr = JSON.stringify(logPayload, null, 2);\n          console.log(\"[AVATAR 3D] Bone Diagnostic Payload:\", logStr);\n          \n          fetch(\"http://localhost:8000/api/debug/logs\", {\n            method: \"POST\",\n            headers: {\n              \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n            },\n            body: JSON.stringify({ logs: logStr }),\n          }).catch(err => console.error(\"Failed to post debug logs:\", err));\n        } catch (e: any) {\n          console.error(\"[AVATAR 3D] Error checking bones:\", e);\n          fetch(\"http://localhost:8000/api/debug/logs\", {\n            method: \"POST\",\n            headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n            body: JSON.stringify({ logs: `Error checking bones: ${e.message}\\nStack: ${e.stack}` }),\n          }).catch(() => {});\n        }"

"### 7. Correcting Reaction Gesture Elbow Flexion Axes & Arm Posing\n- **File**: [Avatar3D.tsx](file:///Users/chandu/Downloads/emotion-agent/frontend/src/app/games/spicy/Avatar3D.tsx)\n- **Fixes**:\n  - Replaced elbow flexion rotations (`leftLowerArm` and `rightLowerArm` joints) from the local Y-axis to the local Z-axis across all dynamic reaction gestures (`cover-mouth`, `folded-arms`, `dominant-smirk`, `shame-blush`).\n  - Switched the bone retrieval inside the animation loop to retrieve raw bone nodes (`getRawBoneNode`) rather than normalized bone nodes (`getNormalizedBoneNode`).\n  - Disabled the automatic normalized-to-raw humanoid copying (`autoUpdateHumanBones = false`) on model load.\n- **Result**: The manual procedural rotations now directly manipulate the raw armature bones that drive the mesh skinning, completely bypassing any normalized bone layer mapping issues or delays. The avatar's arms now bend and move dynamically to match the debug buttons and triggers."
ReplacementContent:
"### 7. Fixing the Mesh-Freeze and Correcting Arm Posing using Normalized Bones\n- **File**: [Avatar3D.tsx](file:///Users/chandu/Downloads/emotion-agent/frontend/src/app/games/spicy/Avatar3D.tsx)\n- **Root Cause**: Direct raw bone manipulation with `autoUpdateHumanBones = false` failed to correctly deform the mesh in Three.js/three-vrm, as the skeletal deforming skin matrices require the humanoid solver to propagate poses. Additionally, calling `vrmModel.update(delta)` at the start of the frame meant the constraints, look-at targets, and spring bones were evaluated on the rest-pose bones instead of the posed skeleton.\n- **Fixes**:\n  - Restored bone retrieval inside the animation loop to use `getNormalizedBoneNode()` and re-enabled automatic normalized-to-raw humanoid copying (`autoUpdateHumanBones = true`) on model load.\n  - **Crucial Loop Sequence Move**: Shifted the `vrmModel.update(delta)` call from the very *beginning* of the `tick()` function to the very *end* of the `if (vrmModel)` block (immediately before `renderer.render()`). This ensures that the custom poses are applied to the normalized bones, then `vrmModel.update(delta)` runs to solve all secondary constraints, lookAt, and spring bones, copying the final posed armature coordinates to the raw bones in a single frame.\n  - Swapped the Z-rotation signs for the upper arms (`leftUpperArm` Z becomes negative to swing down, `rightUpperArm` Z becomes positive to swing down) to match the standardized T-pose bone axes of the VRM humanoid rig.\n- **Result**: The 3D avatar's mesh now deforms correctly in real-time, executing distinct physical gestures (Fold Arms, Smirk/Hands on Hips, Shy/Blush, and Cover Mouth) smoothly when clicking the debug buttons or triggering emotions through chat messages."
